A number of Palestinians including patients were injured.

[EVENT: PSE66469]
Date: 2023-10-31
Country: Palestine | Location: Gaza - Nezarim
Type: Strategic developments > Change to group/activity | Disorder: Strategic developments
Actor1: Military Forces of Israel (2022-) [External/Other forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Anadolu Agency; Haaretz; Times of Israel; Ynet [Regional]

DESCRIPTION:
Security measures: Around 31 October 2023 (month of), Israeli troops established a route connecting the eastern border of the Gaza Strip to the Mediterranean Sea in the west, known as the Nezarim Corridor amid the ground operation in Gaza City (coded to Gaza - Nezarim (Gaza City, Gaza Strip)), begins in Beeri town in Israel and divides the southern areas of Gaza City from the Deir al Balah Governorate. Israeli forces expanded the corridor to cover areas including Wadi Gaza, Juhr ad Dik, Al Mughraqa, Al Zahra, parts of An Nuseirat, Az-Zaytun, Tel al Hawa, and Ash Sheikh Ijlin. Israeli forces established four military posts along the corridor, along with checkpoints, routes, and other military infrastructure. By November 2024, the Nezarim Corridor had reached a width of 5-6 kilometers and a length of approximately 9 kilometers.

[EVENT: PSE39200]
Date: 2023-11-01
Country: Palestine | Location: Jenin
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Al Aqsa Martyrs Brigade [Political militia]
Actor2: Military Forces of Israel (2022-) [External/Other forces]
Fatalities: 3
Sources: Israeli Defense Forces; Ma'an News Agency; Newpress; Palestine Information Center; Palestine News and Information Agency; Quds News Network [Other-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 November 2023, Al Aqsa Martyrs Brigade and Katibat Jenin Palestinian fighters exchanged fire with Israeli forces during a raid in Jenin city (West Bank). Israeli forces fired live bullets and tear gas canisters in several parts of the city including in the vicinity of Jenin public hospital and Mukhayyam Jenin, killing 1 Palestinian and injuring several others. Israeli army bulldozers removed barriers outside Mukhayyam Jenin, as well as parts of its entrance, and conducted excavations on several roads. Israeli drones struck targets in Mukhayyam Jenin , killing 2 Palestinians and injuring another. Israeli forces also destroyed electric transformers, causing a power outage in the city, and seized some Palestinian-owned houses to establish military checkpoints. Katibat Jenin and Al Aqsa Martyrs Brigade militants detonated explosive devices targeting Israeli forces, causing material damages to Israeli bulldozers and vehicles. 3 fatalities.
